# task=oolong_temporal seed=2200002 dataset=agnews qtype=relative_freq gold=['6'] answer='5' term=answered score=0.750
# Q: For how many months does the label 'World' occur more frequently than the label 'Sports'? Disregard months where there is a tie. Give your final answer in the form 'Answer: [X]', where [X] is the number of months where 'World' occurs more often than 'Sports.' Put your final answer inside \boxed{}.
##########################################################################################
============================================================================
[depth=0] turns=2 termination=answered answer='5'
============================================================================
[system] You are a long-document assistant. The document is 2966 tokens long; you cannot see it directly. Your own context window is 3000 tokens — the conversation (system prompt, user message, your responses, and all tool results) must fit in this budget or the episode ends.
